        (0): ModuleDict(
          (attention): MultiheadAttention(
            (W_q): Linear(in_features=8, out_features=8, bias=True)
            (W_k): Linear(in_features=8, out_features=8, bias=True)
            (W_v): Linear(in_features=8, out_features=8, bias=True)
            (W_out): Linear(in_features=8, out_features=8, bias=True)
          )
          (linear0): Linear(in_features=8, out_features=128, bias=True)
          (linear1): Linear(in_features=128, out_features=8, bias=True)
          (norm1): LayerNorm((8,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
        )
      )
      (activation): ReLU()
      (last_activation): ReLU()
      (last_normalization): LayerNorm((8,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
      (head): Linear(in_features=8, out_features=8, bias=True)
    )
  )
  (Reconstructor): Reconstructor(
    (cat_recons): ModuleList(
      (0): Linear(in_features=8, out_features=2, bias=True)
      (1): Linear(in_features=8, out_features=7, bias=True)
      (2): Linear(in_features=8, out_features=4, bias=True)
      (3-6): 4 x Linear(in_features=8, out_features=11, bias=True)
      (7-8): 2 x Linear(in_features=8, out_features=10, bias=True)
    )
  )
)
